BY ADDITIONAL DISTRICT MAGISTRATE
UNDER SECTION 37(1), (3) OF BOMBAY POLICE
ACT, 1951
ORDER
No. Home-Desk 14 (2)-Ws-10-2020.—
Whereas it has been brought to my notice that
it is necessary to prevent commission of certain
acts for peace and public order and safety.
And whereas I am satisfied that it is neces-
sary to prevent commission of certain acts for
the above said purpose.
Now, I, Ravindra Khajanji, Additional District
Magistrate, Nagpur in exercise of the powers
conferred by sub section (1) and (3) of Section 37
of the Bombay Police Act, 1951 (Bom XXII of 1951)
prohibit within the limits of entire Nagpur
District excluding the City of Nagpur.
(1) Assembly or procession of more than five
persons.
(2) The carrying of arms,cudgels, spears, blud-
geons, guns, knives, sticks or lathers of articals
which are capable of being used for causing
physical injury.
(3) The carrying of corrosive substances or
explosives.
(4) The carrying collection and preparation
of stones or other missiles or instruments or
means of a casting of impelling missiles.
This order shall come into force with immedi-
ate effect from 00 01 hours of dated 16th January
2020 and shall remain inforce till 24 00 hours of
dated 30th January 2020.

This order shall be proclaimed by affixing
Receiver
copies at Police Station and at conspicuous places
in the above mentioned area and shall be
published in the Maharashtra Government
Receiver
Gazette.
Given under my hand and seal of Court on this
15th day of January 2020.
RAVINDRA KHAJANJI,
Nagpur : Additional District
Dated the 15th January 2020. Magistrate, Nagpur.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER
MMISF ACT, 2005.
Notification -III
No. 140-TB-WUA-2020.—
Whereas, it has been decided to delineate the area of
operation of Water User’s Associations (WUA’s) on
hydraulic basis and as per Aministrative convenience
under Sections 6, 7 and rule 3 of MMISF Act, 2005. I,
P. M. Phalke, Executive Engineer, Lift Irrigation Project
Division, Tiroda hereby, delineate area of operation of
following WUA’s and direct that the certified copy of the
updated map and list of land holders and /or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the
offices of concern Gram Panchayat, Tahsil Office,
Irrigation Section, IrrigationSub-Division and
Irrigation Division and at other prominent Public
places. ;
Now, therefore, I, P. M. Phalke, Executive Engineer,
Lift Irrigation Project Division, Tiroda also hereby declared
that no water shall be supplied by the Appropriate
Authority to an individual holder or occupant of such land
and the system of supply of water through Water Users
Association shall be binding on all the holders and
occupiers under Maharashtra Management of Irrigation
System by Farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part thereof,
may, within thirty days from the date of publication of this
notification in the Official Gazette, file an appeal before
Superintendent Engineer, Gosikhurd Lift Irrigation Circle,
Ambati (Bhandara).

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER
MMISF ACT, 2005.
Notification-III
No. 240-GRBCDB-Admin-3-WUA-2020.—
Whereas, it has been decided to delineate the area of
Operation of Water User’s Association (WUA’s) on
hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience
under Sections 5, 6, 7 and Rule 3 of MMISF Act, 2005.
I, Roshan Dhanraj Hatwar, Executive Engineer, Gosikhurd
Right Bank Canal Division, Bramhapuri hereby delineate
Area of operation of following WUA’s and direct that the
certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders
and/or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be displayed on
the notice board of the offices of concerned Gram
Panchayat, Tahsil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub-Division
and Division Office, and at other other prominent public
places.
Now, therefore, I, Roshan Dhanraj Hatwar, Executive
Engineer, Gosikhurd Left Bank Canal Division, Bramhapuri
also hereby, declare that no water shall be supplied by
the Appropriate Authority to an individual holder or occu-
piers of such land and system of supply of water through
Water User’s Association shall be binding on all the hold-
ers and occupiers of the lands under Management of Irri-
gation System by Farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part thereof,
may within thirty days from the date of publication of this
notification in the Official Gazette, file and appeal before
Superintending Engineer, Gosikhurd Porject Circle,
Nagpur.

41 L - 124 6 27 6 27 Bramhapuri

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER
MMISF ACT, 2005.
Notification-III
No.241-GRBCDB-Admin-3-WUA-2020.—
Whereas, it has been decided to delineate Area
of Operation of Water User’s Association (WUA’s)
on hydraulic basis and as per Administrative
convenience under Sections 5, 6, 7 and rule 3 of
the MMISF Act, 2005. I, Roshan Dhanraj Hatwar,
Executive Engineer, Gosikhurd Right Bank Canal
Division, Bramhapuri hereby, delineate Area of
Operation of following WUA’s and direct that the
certified copy of the updated map and list of land
holders and/or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be
displayed on the notice board of the offices of
concerned Gram Panchayat, Tahasil Office,
Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and at
other prominent public places;
Now, therefore, I, Roshan Dhanraj Hatwar,
Executive Engineer, Gosikhurd Right Bank Canal
Division, Bramhapuri also hereby declare that no
water shall be supplied by the Appropriate Authority
to an individual holder or occupier of such land and
system of supply of water through Water User’s
Association shall be binding on all the holders and
occupiers of the land under Management of Irriga-
tion System by Farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part
thereof may within thirty days from the date of
publication of this notification in the Official Gazette,
file an appeal before Superintending Engineer,
Gosikhurd Project Circle, Nagpur.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER
MMISF ACT, 2005.
NOTIFICATION-III
No. 289-GRBCDB-Admin-3-WUA-2020.—
Whereas, it has been decided to delineate the Area of
Operation of Water Users Association (WUAs) on
hydraulic basis and as per Administrative convenience
under Sections 5, 6, 7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act,
2005.I, Roshan Dhanraj Hatwar, Executive Engineer,
Gosikhurd Right Bank Canal Division, Bramhapuri hereby,
delineate Area of Operation of following WUAs and direct
that the certified copy of the updated map and list of land
holders and/or occupiers of said WUAs shall be displayed
on the notice board of the offices of concerned Gram
Panchayat, Tahsil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub-Division
and Division and at other Prominent public places.
Now, Therefore, I, Roshan Dhanraj Hatwar, Executive
Engineer, Gosikhurd Right Bank Canal Division,
Bramhapuri also hereby declare that no water shall be
supplied by the Appropriate Authority to an individual holder
or occupier of such land and system of supply of water
through Water User Association shall be binding on all
the holders and occupiers of the land under Management
of Irrigation System by Farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there
of, may within 30 days from the date of publication of this
notification in the Official Gazette, file an appeal before
Superintending Engineer, Gosikhurd Project Circle, Nagpur.

H. A. H. A. MMISF ACT, 2005.—
Notification-III 88 1 03 1 03
No.298-GRBCDB-Admn-3-WUA-2020.— 87 0 95 0 95
Whereas, it has been decided to delineate Area
89 2 08 2 08
of Operation of Water User Association (WUAs)
86 1 10 1 10
on hydraulic basis and as per Administrative
93 1 35 1 35
convenience under Sections 5, 6, 7 and rule 3 of
the MMISF Act, 2005. I, Roshan Dhanraj Hatwar, 94 0 75 0 75
the Executive Engineer, Gosikhurd Right Bank Canal 95 0 74 0 74
Division, Bramhapuri hereby, delineate Area of
85 1 08 1 08
Operation of following WUAs and direct that the
84 0 94 0 94
certified copy of the updated map and list of land
75 1 97 1 97
holders and/or occupiers of said WUAs shall be
73 1 04 1 04 displayed on the notice board of the offices of
concerd Gram Panchayat, Tahsil Office, Irrigation 159 2 00 2 00
Section, Sub Division and Division and at other
81/1 0 46 0 46
82 0 79 0 79
Therefore, I, Roshan Dhanraj Hatwar, the Executive
------------ ------------
Engineer, Gosikhurd Right Bank Canal Division,
Total . . 26 60 26 60
Bramhapuri also hereby declare that no water shall
------------ ------------
be supplied by the Appropriate Authority to an individual
OL-2 90 2 80 2 80
holder or occupier of such land and the system of
RD. 500 M. 106 2 16 2 16
supply of water through Water User Association
105 1 56 1 56
shall be binding on all the holders and occupiers
92/1 0 75 0 75
of the land under Management of Irrigation System
92/2 0 60 0 60 by Farmers.
91 0 43 0 43 Any person affected by this notification or part
thereof, may, within thirty days from the date of 100 0 68 0 68
publication of this notification in the Official Gazette, 101 0 70 0 70
file an appeal before Superintending Engineer,
99 0 48 0 48
Gosikhurd Project Circle, Nagpur.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER
MMISF ACT, 2005.
Notification-III
No. 310- GRBCDB-Admin-3-WUA-2020.—
Whereas, it has been decided to delineate the area
of Operation of Water User’s Association (WUA’s) on
hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under
Sections 5, 6, 7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act, 2005. I, Roshan
Dhanraj Hatwar, Executive Engineer, Gosikhurd Right Bank
Canal Division, Bramhapuri hereby, delineate area of Opera-
tion of following WUA’s and direct that the certified copy of
the updated map and list of land holders and/or occupiers of
said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the
offices of concerned Gram Panchayat, Tahsil Office, Irrigation
Section, Sub-Division and Division and at other prominent
public places.

Therefore, I, Roshan Dhanraj Hatwar, Executive Engi- SCHEDULE—Contd.E
neer, Gosikhurd Right Bank Canal Division, Bramhapuri also (1) (2) (3) (4)
hereby, declare that no water shall be supplied by the Appro- H. A. H. A.
priate Authority to an individual holder or occupiers of such
D.OL-5 173 0 12 0 12
land and the system of supply of water through Water User’s
R.D. 5053 M. 174 2 93 2 93
Association shall be binding on all the holders and occupiers
175 0 59 0 59 of the lands under Management of Irrigation System by
Farmers. 176/1 0 41 0 41
176/2 0 69 0 69 Any person affected by this notification or part thereof,
may within thirty days from the date of publication of this 130 0 00 2 46
notification in the Official Gazette, file an appeal before
131 0 00 0 12
Superintending Engineer, Gosikhurd Project Circle, Nagpur.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER
MMISF ACT, 2005.—
Notification-III
No. 312-GRBCDB-Admin-3-2020.—
Whereas, it has been decided to delineate the Area
of Operation of Water User Associations (WUA’s) on
hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience
under Sections 5, 6, 7 and rule 3 of the MMISF ACT
2005. I, Roshan Dhanraj Hatwar, Executive Engineer,
Gosikhurd Right Bank Canal Division, Bramhapuri,
hereby, delineate Area of operation of following WUA’s
and direct that the certified copy of the updated map and
list of land holders and/or occupiers of said WUA’s shall

SCHEDULE—Contd.
be displayed on the notice board of the Gram Panchayat,
Tahsil Offices, Irrigation Section, Sub-Division and (1) (2) (3) (4)
Division & at other Prominent public places.
H. A. H. A.

Engineer, Gosikhurd Right Bank Canal Division, 188 1 99 1 99
Bramhapuri, also hereby, declare that no water shall be

supplied by the Appropriate Authority to an individuals

holder or occupier of such land and the system of supply

of Water through Users Association shall be binding on

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER
MMISF ACT, 2005.
Notification-III
No. 386-DB-Noti-3-2020.—
Whereas, it has been decided to delineate Area of
Operation of Water User’s Association (WUA’s) on
hydraulic basis and as per Administrative convenience
under Sections 5, 6, 7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF
Act, 2005. I, Jitendra Turkhede, Executive Engineer,
Pench Irrigation Division, Nagpur hereby, delineate
Areas of Operation of following WUAs and direct that the
certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders
and/or occupiers of said WUAs shall be displayed on
the notice board of the offices of concerned Gram
Panchayat, Tahsil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub-Divi-
sion and Division and at other prominent public places ;
Now therefore, I, Jitendra Turkhede, Executive
Engineer, Pench Irrigation Division, Nagpur also hereby
declare that no water should be supplied by the Appro-
priate Authority to an individual holder or occupier of such
land and the system of supply of water through Water
User’s Association shall be binding on all the holders
and occupiers of the land under Management of Irrigation
System by Farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part thereof,
may within 30 days from the date of publication of this

SCHEDULE—Contd. notification in the Official Gazette, file an appeal before
Superintending Engineer, Command Area Development
(1) (2) (3)
Authority, Nagpur.
H. A.

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER
MMISF ACT, 2005.
Notification-III
No.363–Admin -EGS–WUA–Noti.-3-2020.—
Whereas, it has been decided to delineate Area of Opera-
tion of Water User Association (WUAs) on hydraulic basis
and as per administrative convenience under Sections 5, 6, 7
and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act, 2005. I, Devanand Kacharu
Manvatakar, Executive Engineer, Bhandara Irrigation Divi-
sion, Bhandara hereby, delineate Area of operation of follow-
ing WUAs and direct that the certified copy of the updated
map and list of land holders and/or occupiers of said WUAs
shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of con-
cerned Gram Panchayat, Tahsil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub-
Division and Division and at other prominent public places .
Therefore I, Devanand Kacharu Manvatakar , Executive
Engineer,Bhandara Irrigation Division, Bhandara, also hereby,
declare that no water shall be supplied by the Appropriate
Authority to an individual holder or occupiers of such land
and the system of supply of water through Water Users
Association shall be binding on all the holders and occupiers
of land under Management of Irrigation System by Farmers.
